stage4_gemma4_routing_receipt: "2026-09-02 15:39 to 16:0x KST: getting a NON-thinking Gemma 4 in front of the proxy took four attempts, and the finding generalises. The model card says thinking is off unless <|think|> opens the system prompt, and a raw prompt without that token answers in Korean at once (오늘 꿈 얘기 들어볼게). But every Ollama route turned thinking on for a proxy-shaped request, which never carries the think field: (1) the library tag gemma4:e2b (6.67 GiB with the vision/audio projector bundled) — thinking field filled with an English analysis, content empty at 96 tokens; (2) a derived tag with an explicit Gemma-turn TEMPLATE and no think token — Ollama keeps the parent native RENDERER gemma4 / PARSER gemma4, which take precedence over TEMPLATE, same leak; (3) a tag built FROM the official text-only QAT q4_0 GGUF (3,349,516,256 bytes, sha256 fa401b55b07ee70a54c6dae3903c783a6e65064312529ea57175cb5f8dec6634, GGUF magic verified, tag digest f664c2f6) — Ollama 0.32.6 attaches the gemma4 renderer by architecture even for a file source, same leak. The hf.co pull with a :Q4_0 tag was rejected by the repository (400, tag not available), which is why the file was fetched directly; it is downloadable without a token. ROUTE THAT WORKS: the Stage 2 evaluation shim (ollama-proxy/eval/llama_server_shim.py, unchanged) on 11434 fronting the Ollama-bundled llama-server on 11500 loading the text-only GGUF with --jinja --reasoning-budget 0 and enable_thinking false. Ollama serve was stopped first with nothing loaded (11434 free, no orphan). A direct /v1/chat/completions probe answered 어제 꾼 꿈은 완전 대박이었지! with no reasoning_content, and the proxy-shaped probe THROUGH the shim answered 오늘 꿈은 뭐였어? with an empty thinking field — clean. GPU DETAIL WORTH KEEPING: the bundled llama-server.exe reports no devices from its own directory, because ggml discovers backend DLLs relative to the working directory; started with cwd set to lib/ollama/cuda_v12 it lists CUDA0 RTX 3060 Ti, and the GGML_BACKEND_PATH variable does not work on this build. Measured: CPU 18.1 tok/s with VRAM flat at 1,130 MiB, versus CUDA 45.1 tok/s with VRAM 2,823 MiB after the model loaded. The evaluation now runs on the CUDA instance; the shim reports the pinned name gemma4-airi-eval:e2b-text and digest f664c2f6 on /api/tags so the launcher preflight and proxy digest pin bind normally. Sampling parity: the proxy stamps 0.45/0.9/1.05 per request as for every other candidate; llama-server defaults do not set top_k 64 the way the Ollama Modelfile did, so no extra knob differs. No adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
stage4_gemma4_intent: "2026-09-02 15:1x KST: user asked to try Gemma 4 as a fourth model-axis candidate. Facts verified before pulling: the family exists (E2B, E4B, 12B, 26B-A4B, 31B) on HF and the Ollama library; the model card states Apache 2.0, so it is license-clean like Qwen3 and eligible for a later adoption discussion; thinking is OFF by default and enabled only by a <|think|> token at the start of the system prompt, which the immutable AIRI prompt does not contain, so the qwen3 thinking trap does not apply but will still be probed; the card claims 140-plus languages without naming Korean, so item 8 is unknown until measured. Official QAT q4_0 text GGUFs: E2B 3.35 GB, E4B 5.15 GB (4.5B effective, 8B with embeddings), 12B 7.6 GB on Ollama. E2B is chosen first because it fits the 8 GiB card with the 4096 context comfortably; E4B is borderline and is deferred to a second round only if E2B shows a Korean or coherence gain worth chasing. Source is the official google GGUF repository through the hf.co pull path (text-only q4_0 file; the mmproj vision/audio projector is not needed for this text harness), with the Ollama library gemma4:e2b as fallback. DESIGN unchanged from the other three candidates: single variable is the model; M8-9 run-01 form, spine sidecar, unchanged cue, 32 turns, validators, knowledge 153/296, temperature 0.45, launcher -ChatModel plus -ChatModelDigest path, operational tag untouched; run bare without the Stage 3 adapter. PRE-REGISTERED EXPECTATION: items 2, 3 and 7 hold by the spine; items 4, 5 and 6 will fail in the un-aligned baseline class as every bare candidate has, because that residual was structural across 2.1b to 7b; the one place Gemma 4 could beat the field is item 8 Korean naturalness versus qwen3-4b-instruct and item 1 specificity versus kanana. A result better than Mi:dm plus Stage 3 (5 of 7) is not expected. No adoption, no commit and no push."

stage4_adelie_receipt: "2026-09-02 11:16 KST: adelie-7B (hf.co/ramyun/adelie-qwen-roleplay-v2-gguf:Q4_K_M, digest d99cb2b2) ran the full 32 turns in spine mode, runner exit 0, external root 20260902-vod-storyline-s4-adelie, report sha256 df208869f0abf23fb001ca9d7dd52339472888e2ac323cf5f50634a4cc50d61. The wrapper script reported exit 1 AFTER the run, and that was cosmetic: ollama stop paints ANSI progress glyphs on stderr and PowerShell 5.1 under ErrorActionPreference Stop promotes native stderr to a terminating error even at exit 0. Teardown still completed — proxy down, llama-server 0, no model loaded, VRAM 937 MiB — and the report is whole. The two Stage 4 scripts now route ollama stop through cmd.exe so it cannot recur. FLOORS pass mechanically (32/32, 8/8 ordered, signatures 0, errors 0, rewrites 32/32, repeated 0, similarity 0.056, spine echo 0 with a longest shared run of 4), with one draft-path fallback at T01 — the first sign of the CPU-era first-token deadline on this PC, plausibly the 7B first load. My regexes read own 0, honorific 0, 제일 0, question 1, English 0, average length 44 (the longest of any run), 28 distinct 4-char openers. MANUAL REVIEW: HARD FAIL, the worst generated sentences of any configuration despite three times the parameters. The failure class is new — INCOHERENCE and HALLUCINATION, not register: T02 records the sound of emptying red bricks, T10 refuses to kneel and spread scale cards on a rainy night, T19 has an inner lock and a blinking red light, T20 a young Dongle in the mirror, T32 a withdrawal page written in numbers. Broken Korean appears (달짝지케, 이래뵈다, 안전한 곳에 머물러 있어야 해 as a stiff command), which is exactly the eighth item, Korean naturalness, failing outright. Old defects return in fresh surface forms the regexes miss: advice in T05 (go get examined), T08, T30 (recommends watching together), T31; role inversion with 너 in T17 and T27 (who gave you the right to stick with the group); first-person ownership in T12, T15 and T24 as a diary-keeping streamer. Spine sentences remain correct by construction, so the collapse is confined to the single generated sentence, as before — but here that sentence is not merely off-register, it is off-story. Items 1, 4, 5, 6 and 8 fail; only 2, 3 and 7 hold and those are the spine own. READING: an RP fine-tune of a 7B base without Korean-alignment quality is not a step up from Mi:dm 2.3B plus Stage 3 on this task; size did not buy coherence, and the fine-tune bought a diary-streamer persona that fights the story-performer framing. License already unresolved, so this candidate is closed on two counts. Next: think probe on the Qwen3 Instruct evaluation tag, then kanana-nano as the size control, then qwen3-4b-instruct. No adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
stage4_pull_and_qwen3_think_receipt: "2026-09-02 11:0x to 11:2x KST: all three candidates pulled with exit 0 — qwen3:4b (Ollama library, 2.33 GiB, digest 359d7dd4), adelie hf.co/ramyun/adelie-qwen-roleplay-v2-gguf:Q4_K_M (4.36 GiB, digest d99cb2b2), kanana-nano hf.co/DevQuasar/kakaocorp.kanana-nano-2.1b-instruct-GGUF:Q4_K_M (1.30 GiB, digest 0c377999; DevQuasar is the same GGUF publisher the repository already trusts for Mi:dm). Stock templates inspected: adelie carries a plain ChatML template with no thinking scaffold; kanana carries a single-turn ChatML Prompt/Response template with im_start and im_end stops; both are runnable as-is through -ChatModel. QWEN3:4B IS BLOCKED BY THINKING, AND EVERY DOCUMENTED SWITCH FAILED — measured, not assumed. Its stock template ends the generation prompt with an opened think tag, so a proxy-shaped /api/chat request (the proxy never sends the think field) spent all 96 tokens in the thinking field and returned EMPTY content. Attempts, each with the exact proxy sampling options: (1) a derived tag qwen3-airi-eval:4b-nothink (digest 1e573773) whose template prefills the empty think block Qwen documents for non-thinking mode — the model went on reasoning in English as plain content; (2) raw-mode prefill of both empty-block variants — same; (3) the /no_think soft switch in the user turn and in the system turn — thinking field still filled, response empty; (4) Ollama-native think:false as a reference — the model then emitted its English reasoning as CONTENT (Okay, the user wants me to act as a radio host...). So with a Korean broadcast-persona system prompt this build reasons first no matter what, and under the harness SLOT_MAX_TOKENS of 96 the answer never arrives; parse_slot_output would reject every candidate for English and meta and the slot pool would exhaust on every turn. Running it would measure the thinking behaviour, not the model axis. RESOLUTION IN PROGRESS: pull the non-thinking Qwen3 Instruct build (the 2507 instruct-only line, still Apache 2.0) and re-probe; qwen3:8b is deferred because it shares the thinking design. The adelie run was started meanwhile because it is independent of the qwen3 question. Nothing adopted, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
m8_8_stage4_intent: "2026-09-02 00:3x KST: user decision — evaluate all three model-axis candidates FOR RESEARCH ONLY. The roadmap resume condition for M8-8 (Stages 1 to 3 exhausted and still no 3-consecutive pass) is literally satisfied, and the temperature ablation closed the last untried sampling variable. LICENSE BOUNDARY, verified against the actual license documents before anything was pulled and recorded here so it cannot be forgotten: Mi:dm 2.0 mini is MIT (local LICENSE.txt); qwen3 4b/8b is Apache 2.0 (HF card) and is the only candidate eligible for any later adoption discussion; kanana-nano-2.1b is CC-BY-NC-4.0 (HF card), NON-COMMERCIAL, so it is a diagnostic size-class control only and can never be adopted; adelie-qwen-roleplay-v2 is labelled tongyi-qianwen by its author while its base Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct is actually Apache 2.0 on HF, so its license is unresolved and it is evaluation-only until the author clarifies. The local kanana-2-3b is a different model under the custom Kanana Open License (own-service use allowed, Powered-by-Kanana display and Notice duties, resale needs Kakao approval) and is not one of the three. adoption_authorized stays false for all of them. DESIGN: single variable is the model. Everything else is the run-01 M8-9 form — spine sidecar speaking the two plot slots, model writing only viewer_reaction, unchanged cue, storyline, 32 staged turns, validators, knowledge 153/296, temperature 0.45. Each candidate goes through the launcher supported -ChatModel plus -ChatModelDigest evaluation path; the operational midm-airi:2.0-mini tag is untouched. Manual review gains an eighth explicit item, Korean naturalness, because Korean ability was the reason the model axis was deferred. Candidates are evaluated bare, without the Stage 3 adapter, which is Mi:dm-specific; alignment-shaped defects in a candidate are therefore expected and are not a fail on their own, since the Stage 3 recipe is known to close them. What decides the axis is whether the candidate resolves the residual that four Mi:dm configurations could not: one free reaction sentence without spending item 4, 5 or 6. SOURCES: qwen3:4b from the Ollama library; kanana-nano-2.1b and adelie from hf.co GGUF repositories through the same hf.co pull path the repository already uses for Mi:dm. Qwen3 thinking mode must be off (roadmap says think-off); the proxy never sends the think request field, so a derived evaluation tag will carry a Modelfile template that prefills the empty think block Qwen documents for non-thinking mode, verified by a probe before any run. Pulls are explicit evaluation actions by this session, not launcher behaviour — normal startup never pulls. Order: qwen3:4b first, then adelie, then kanana as the size control. No training, no adoption, no commit and no push."
m89_temperature_ablation_receipt: "2026-09-02 00:1x KST: temperature 0.2 run completed, exit 0, external root 20260901-vod-storyline-m89-r4-temp02, report sha256 bd1a8fdb30084966b3ac55527883c4b2976813f21d245ea635e95864b30afff. Floors all pass (32/32, 8/8 ordered, signatures 0, errors 0, rewrites 32/32, repeated 0, similarity 0.053), spine echo 0 exact and 0 near with a longest shared run of 7. LEVER DISCARDED: not better than r4 at 0.45, and the pre-registered risk materialised. What moved the right way: the 제일 tic fell from 10 to 6 turns and 제일 먼저 from 5 to 2, average length 38 to 36. What moved the wrong way, read from the 32 sentences rather than from regexes: reactions flattened into generic filler (T04 says this happens often, T13 says that is fun, T01 says it will take a while), advice register crept back in T07 and T14, first-person feeling bleed reappeared in about four turns under NEW surface forms my ownership regex does not catch — T19 feels someone pacing outside and gets anxious, T26 the lonely feeling came first, T29 cannot bear it alone, T32 despair swept in first — so the regex 0 is an under-count and manual reading puts item 6 slightly worse than r4, which was clean on both. T10 also dropped a syllable (음이랑 for 입안이랑) and pre-announced the nodule the spine says next. Net: item 4 improved a little, items 5 and 6 regressed a little, item 1 got flatter; not a configuration to keep. Temperature 0.45 remains the setting. THE SHARPER FINDING, now visible across four configurations: run-01, repro, r4 and temp02 all land on exactly 5 of 7, and the authored spine READ ALONE scores 6 of 7 — it fails only item 1 by construction because it never addresses the viewer, while items 2, 3, 5, 6 and 7 hold by validation and item 4 holds because the 64 lines were authored distinct. So the single generated sentence has had a NET CONTRIBUTION OF ZERO in every configuration tried: it buys item 1 and spends one of items 4, 5 or 6 every time, with only which one changing. That reframes the residual: it is not a defect to be chased slot by slot, it is the price of letting a 2.3B model speak one free sentence next to authored text. Options that follow from that, all user decisions: (a) make item 1 cheaper for the model — a short authored reaction opener pool the runner samples deterministically, with the model filling only a clause, which is more authoring and less model; (b) Stage 4 model axis, whose roadmap resume condition is now literally satisfied; (c) accept 6 of 7 as the product answer and drop the generated sentence, treating viewer reaction as a separate lightweight path rather than part of the scripted reply. Proxy was started directly for this run and is being stopped through its normal path with the model unloaded by ollama stop, not by force. No training, no adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
m89_temperature_ablation_intent: "2026-09-02 00:0x KST: next lever after r4, chosen because it is the one sampling variable this entire track never varied across 80-plus runs. Single variable versus the r4 spine run: temperature 0.45 to 0.2, with top_p 0.9 and repeat_penalty 1.05, model midm-airi-s3:eval-r4, spine sidecar, cue, storyline, knowledge 153/296 and validators all unchanged. Mechanism verified before the run: the proxy applies OLLAMA_SAMPLING_DEFAULTS with setdefault only, and start-local-ollama-proxy.ps1 exposes -OllamaTemperature, so no proxy code changed; the stack launcher does not forward that parameter, so the proxy was started directly with the same knowledge, memory, model and digest arguments the stack path passes. /health reports temperature 0.2, top_p 0.9, repeat_penalty 1.05, knowledge 153/296, digest pinned. Why this and not a spine-only ablation: the spine never addresses the viewer, so a spine-only reply fails manual item 1 by construction — that ablation is answered without a run. The generated sentence is therefore load-bearing for exactly item 1, and the residual defects (the 제일 tic, fabrication, pre-announcing the spine) are all degrees of freedom inside that one sentence, which is what temperature directly constrains. Pre-registered expectation: the 제일 tic and fabrication should fall, item 1 should hold, and the risk is flattening — a lower temperature may make the reaction generic and cost item 1. External root 20260901-vod-storyline-m89-r4-temp02. No training, no adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."

ITEM 6 IS CLOSED BY TRAINING. Borrowed-experience expressions in the generated slot went 2 (run-01), 3 (repro), 0 (r4). The sentences that carried it in r3 — resting its own high notes, tasting the candy, being unable to breathe — are gone and replaced by third-person commentary (T13 calls it this person taste, T17 this person life, T27 that person mind). Three cue interventions failed to touch this; one training round closed it. ITEM 4 FAILS AGAIN, AND FOR THE THIRD TIME THE CAUSE IS MY OWN DATA. v3 targets leaned on 제일 (제일 서늘한 부분, 제일 뼈아파, 제일 궁금한 대목), and the adapter amplified it: 제일 now appears in 10 of 32 turns and 제일 먼저 in 5, against 3 and 1 at run-01. This is the same failure as v1 (agreement openers) and v2 (emotion labels): a 32-slot corpus is small enough that my vocabulary habits are imprinted wholesale, which is the same root as the dev loss bottoming at epoch 1 every time. Generated sentences also grew from 31 to 38 characters, and new defects appeared — T23 invents having nothing to wear against a beat where the character is already dressed, T24 dismisses the viewer question the spine is about to answer, T29 asks the viewer a question, and T19 and T21 pre-announce what the spine is about to say. VERDICT: still 5 of 7. The passing set merely rotated — item 6 now passes and item 4 fails, where before item 6 failed. Consecutive quality passes remain 0/3 and nothing is adopted. CONCLUSION RECORDED SO THE NEXT ROUND IS NOT A FOURTH REPEAT: changing only the tic again would be the fourth cycle of the same mole-whack. The two honest options are (a) enlarge the correction corpus with genuinely different slots, which needs another authored storyline and is a real authoring cost, or (b) freeze M8-9 v0 at 5 of 7 as the reached point and reclassify the remaining two items as authoring and structure problems rather than model problems. No adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."

m89_kill_cause_correction_receipt: "2026-09-01 21:48 KST: MY EARLIER DIAGNOSIS WAS WRONG AND IS CORRECTED HERE. The m89_vram_contention_receipt above attributed the two external kills to VRAM contention on the 8 GiB card. The user pushed back that the constraint was system memory, not the GPU, and measurement proved the user right: 31.93 GiB total with only 3.65 GiB free (11.4 percent) and commit charge at 41.19 of 52.93 GiB. The earlier entry was also internally inconsistent, because I had already recorded that M8-9 does not need a GPU and then blamed the GPU for the interruptions. ROOT CAUSE, AND IT WAS MINE: two ORPHANED llama-server.exe processes were holding the memory, PID 20020 from the eval-r2 weights blob and PID 25092 from the eval-r3 blob at 6,262 MB resident. Both parents were the Ollama servers I had force-terminated with Stop-Process to free VRAM before training; Ollama spawns llama-server as its model runner and a forced parent kill leaves the runner alive and parentless. So the interruptions were not a harness defect, not a GPU limit, and not the user gaming — they were my own leftovers starving the machine. CLEANUP RECEIPT: both orphans were terminated. Free RAM went from 3.5 GiB to 15.13 GiB, that is 11.63 GiB recovered and 11.4 percent to 47.4 percent free, and VRAM fell from 4,728 to 2,207 MiB as a side effect. A follow-up scan found no remaining AIRI process and no owned port bound, so the stack footprint was zero; the top memory consumers left were the user own applications. OPERATIONAL RULE TO CARRY FORWARD: never force-kill the Ollama server to reclaim memory. Unload the model and stop the server through its normal path, and before declaring a session idle, check explicitly for surviving llama-server.exe processes, because a single orphan holds GB-scale RAM indefinitely and will silently kill long background runs. The stack was then restarted cleanly for the reproduction run with 16.28 GiB free, /health ok, knowledge 153/296, evaluation tag digest pinned. No adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
m89_vram_contention_receipt: "2026-09-01 21:45 KST: the cause of both external kills is identified and it is not a defect in the harness. The user reported gaming on this machine, and measurement agrees: this is an 8,192 MiB card carrying 29 CUDA compute processes with 6,190 MiB already in use while the evaluation model alone holds 2,274,214,542 bytes resident. The two reproduction attempts died at turn 22 and turn 13 under that contention. The stack was therefore stopped to give the card back: VRAM fell from 6,190 to 4,573 MiB, ports 11434, 11435 and 8892 are free, and no AIRI python process remains. The 4,573 MiB still in use belongs to non-AIRI workloads. CONSEQUENCE FOR THE RECORD: the M8-9 5-of-7 result from run-01 stands on ONE completed run and has NOT been reproduced. The 3-consecutive verdict is not started and must not be claimed. Nothing about that measurement is in doubt — the run completed with exit 0 and its report is preserved — but reproduction is outstanding purely because the card was busy. OPTIONS RECORDED FOR THE USER, none taken unilaterally: (a) re-run the restored run-01 form when the GPU is free, about 4 minutes; (b) run the same thing CPU-only through the launcher OllamaNumGpu 0 path, which leaves the weights and the harness identical and only costs wall clock, since spine mode issues 16 model calls per turn instead of 48; (c) pause M8-9 at v0 and consolidate. Whichever is chosen, the next quality lever after reproduction is the viewer_reaction-only corrective training round aimed at item 6, and that one does need the GPU. No adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."

m89_cue_levers_discarded_receipt: "2026-09-01 21:29 KST: three cue interventions were tried against the M8-9 residual and ALL THREE ARE DISCARDED BY MEASUREMENT, which restores run-01 as the best configuration at 5 of 7 manual items. (1) run-02, spine text shown in the viewer_reaction cue (report sha256 1ce746041a760be584699c7c768c386be377d4685cbce3b79ebb405b2c61e5ed, 4.5 min): the model COPIED it — 4 verbatim and 4 near-verbatim of 32 turns, 9 turns sharing at least 14 contiguous characters with a spine line and a longest run of 50, so those turns said the same sentence twice in a row and item 4 got worse than run-01, which had 0 copies and a longest run of 8. (2) run-03, same cue plus the spine recitation guard (report sha256 5712c97d0cd8212dacbac9a47848f440ec3ddbfd1b249b930037ebb80cebd326, 4.4 min): the guard worked exactly and cost nothing — 0 exact copies, 0 turns at or above the threshold, longest run 12, candidate pool healthy at 468 of 512 slot calls parsing and zero turns left without a valid slot — but the content came straight back as PARAPHRASE on roughly eight turns (T02, T03, T04, T06, T07, T09, T11, T16 each restate the sentence that follows them), so item 4 still fails and item 6 was unchanged, with T05 flipping the spine water glass into painkillers and T14 producing advice about dodging the doctor. (3) run-04, spine text NOT shown and only the slot role narrowed by instruction (report sha256 910545b2252ee9060220f66437b829cd1271a9aee3bd2fefc788272011a62a5d, 3.8 min): echo returned to run-01 levels (0 copies, longest run 8) but borrowed experience was unchanged at about five turns and CONTRADICTIONS ROSE from two turns to four (T19 asserts someone really was outside the door where the beat only has suspicion, T24 invents the day before the event, T30 is incoherent, T32 has the character coming out of the equipment they were just captured by). Not better than run-01. THE PATTERN IS NOW THIRD-TIME REPRODUCED and should be treated as a fixed property of this stack: material OR instruction added to this cue makes a 2.3B model worse, because it treats anything in the cue as something to say rather than as context to respect. The prior two reproductions are the M8-3 demo leakage and the M8-4 depth injection. The judgement is recorded in code as a comment and pinned by a test asserting the generated slot cue stays byte-identical to the slotwise carrier and that no spine sentence can reach the prompt. KEPT from the discarded rounds: the spine recitation guard, because it is the existing 14-character beat-recitation rule extended to text that is now director text, it was measured to cost nothing, and it closes an accidental-recitation hole even when the spine is not shown. Verification across all edits: py_compile clean, broadcast_sim 269 passed / 1 skipped, git diff --check clean, CRLF normalised after each append. REMAINING PATH FOR ITEM 6: cue changes are exhausted. The only coherent option left is a viewer_reaction-only corrective training round targeting the observed borrowed-experience sentences, which the now-standing pipeline makes cheap at roughly 30 minutes training plus 5 packaging plus 4 evaluation. Making the reaction slot deterministic too would script the whole reply and defeat the purpose of the track. No adoption, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
m89_spine_run01_receipt: "2026-09-01 21:08 KST: M8-9 first run, exit 0, 4.1 minutes, external root 20260901-vod-storyline-m89-spine, report sha256 016623c7f85c4b8f823e72c5f67ce62ad70198dd3a709707c85655521c909a0d, model midm-airi-s3:eval-r3 (the Stage 3 adapter) so the spine sits on top of the alignment fixes rather than replacing them. IMPLEMENTATION: new sidecar ollama-proxy/eval/broadcast_sim/vod_storyline_20260827_spine.json holds 32 turns of authored event_callback_emotion and next_hook lines. They are not new writing: they are the Stage 3 correction targets, already authored as in-story sentences, with KEEP entries resolved to baseline model output that manual review had accepted. The pinned storyline file is untouched. The runner gains --rewrite-format spine and --spine; in that mode the two plot-carrying slots are spoken from the sidecar and only viewer_reaction is generated, which cut model calls per turn from 48 to 16 and wall clock from 9-12 minutes to 4.1. load_spine holds the spine to the SAME contract as model output: every line must survive parse_slot_output against its own beat, and a test proves an advice-register line is rejected from the spine exactly as it would be from the model. The report now records spine_sha256. Verification: py_compile clean, storyline suite 22 passed (4 new), broadcast_sim 267 passed / 1 skipped, git diff --check clean, mixed line endings introduced by the append were corrected to CRLF. Honest note on process: the tests were written in the same batch as the implementation, so only the loader-rejection test is a genuine behavioural red/green. FLOORS ALL PASS: 32/32, 8/8 ordered, signatures 0, errors 0, rewrites 32/32, repeated 0, similarity 0.092, fallback drafts 0. MANUAL 32-TURN 7-ITEM REVIEW: FIVE OF SEVEN NOW PASS, which is the best result this project has produced. Item 1 concrete viewer reaction passes, item 2 new event and callback passes because the spine carries it correctly on all 32 turns, item 3 emotion in dialogue passes with enacted beats throughout (fingertips fidgeting, gripping the quilt, biting the lip, clutching the throat, setting down the glass, hat pulled low, knees braced), item 5 staging and advice passes, item 7 irrelevant knowledge and signatures passes. Objective markers: question turns 2, honorific 0, advice 1, demo leakage 0, ellipsis 0, third-person subject 29 of 32. THE TWO REMAINING FAILURES ARE BOTH IN THE ONE SENTENCE THE MODEL STILL WRITES, and they have a single structural cause: the viewer_reaction cue does not contain the spine text, so the model reacts blind to what it is about to say. Item 6 borrowed experience FAILS on about four turns where the model speaks as if AIRI owns the story body — T05 says its throat hurts more today, T10 and T12 say it will rest its high notes, T13 says it could not eat the candy because its throat hurt, which also CONTRADICTS the spine sentence that follows saying the candy relaxed the hand. T24 invents that the outfit had been worn at the event before, and T30 inverts the rule by saying watching together made stopping harder. Item 4 repetition is marginal: T26 model sentence and the spine next_hook say the same stay-close idea, and a new opener tic appeared with 특히 on T08, T09 and T14. READING: the architecture inversion did exactly what it was designed to do. Fabrication, beat contradiction, subject inversion and missing new events are gone from the two plot slots by construction, and every residual is confined to the single generated sentence. The next lever is therefore one variable and structurally implied by the new design rather than mole-whacking: give the viewer_reaction cue the two spine sentences that will follow, and require the slot to react to the viewer without repeating or contradicting them. Adoption stays false, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
gpu_pc_stage3_r3_receipt: "2026-09-01 19:57 KST: r3 trained (exit 0, 27.1 minutes) and evaluated (exit 0, 9.3 minutes) on the same fixed 32 turns. External root 20260901-vod-storyline-s3-r3, report sha256 ee74c2afae2994138bf7eada8e7739f6a12a7d01b3124d401cb577bfa43ffa60, evaluation tag midm-airi-s3:eval-r3 digest 8bdde378, created from a Modelfile that again made ollama create reuse the operational template and params blobs, so the weights layer stays the only difference. Dev curve repeats r2 exactly in shape: minimum at epoch 1 (2.6585), rising at epoch 2 and 3, dev-selected state published, so the effective dose is again about one epoch. Floors all pass: 32/32, 8/8 ordered, signatures 0, errors 0, rewrites 32/32, repeated 0, similarity 0.061, fallback drafts 0. ALL THREE PRE-REGISTERED PREDICTIONS HELD. (1) Alignment stays closed and improved slightly: question-mark turns 20 to 0 (r2 was 2), advice 8 to 0, honorific 3 to 0, demo leakage 4 hits to 0, second person 1 to 0, ellipsis 2 to 0, and the held-out turns 6, 14, 22 and 30 moved identically, with literal reuse of the 184 authored targets at exact 0 and near 3. (2) Items 3 and 4 improved but only partly: the induced agreement-opener formula is GONE, which was the point of the revision, and emotion is now frequently enacted rather than named (T10 shuts the mouth with a flustered face, T06 gets up with worry and disbelief mixed, T18 sighs, T28 waves a hand and looks away, T31 stiffens into despair); but WITHIN-turn idea repetition survives and in places got worse (T02 repeats the sharpened-chase idea in sentences 2 and 3, T18 repeats the vanished-detail idea in all three, T26 says the same stay-close idea in sentences 1 and 3, T32 repeats both the unknown-equipment phrase and the look-away phrase). (3) Item 2 did NOT improve and in fact got WORSE, exactly as pre-registered. Fabrication and subject inversion are now pervasive: T13 invents jelly and carbonation then contradicts itself with a sour taste in the same turn, T14 produces the same nonsense as r2 about a doctor and the candy, T15 asserts the recovery routine began with the dream, T16 has the CANDY feel ominous curiosity, T19 and T20 invert who sees whom, T21 invents a makeup room, T24 invents the day before the event and a nonsensical reason for running out, T27 says the person followed the companions when the beat says they separated, T29 makes the PURSUER the one who works out the rule, T30 inverts the rule to people scattering meaning it cannot move, and T32 invents a door opening. MANUAL 32-TURN 7-ITEM REVIEW FAILS; consecutive passes remain 0/3. THE TRADE-OFF IS THE FINDING, and it is what makes this the wall rather than one more failed round. v1 targets were simple: comprehension held up relatively well but style became formulaic. v2 targets embedded an action beat to show emotion, which made them longer and compositionally harder: style diversified and comprehension degraded. Two opposite data designs, each fixing what the other broke, and NEITHER passes. Crucially, item 2 was already failing in r2, the better of the two on that axis, so even a hypothetical middle design that combined v1 comprehension with v2 style would still fail the manual review. That is why no further correction-data variant is run: the reachable best case does not pass. This is consistent with the Small Model Learnability Gap direction — longer, more compositionally complex targets hurt a 2.3B model — and it is now measured locally rather than cited. CONCLUSION FOR M8-7: corrective fine-tuning DELIVERED on the alignment axis that four inference-time levers and full serving-layer control could never touch — advice, honorifics, question endings, demo dependence, role inversion and source experience are closed, with held-out transfer and near-zero memorisation proving it is learned behaviour rather than recall. It CANNOT deliver on the capability axis: holding the beat without contradicting it, keeping subjects straight, inventing a new event that is actually in the storyline, and not repeating one idea across three sentences. Those need comprehension this base does not have, and 96 distinct corrected slots cannot install it. Stage 3 is therefore EXHAUSTED as a path to a manual pass, and the next method is M8-9, the deterministic spine, which removes the comprehension requirement from the model instead of trying to teach it. Stage 4, the model axis, remains last by user priority. No adoption, adoption_authorized stays false, the operational tag is untouched, no commit and no push."
gpu_pc_stage3_r3_intent: "2026-09-01 19:28 KST: r3 pushes training one round further before any architecture decision, because the two defects that got worse in r2 were mine, not the model. SINGLE CAUSE versus r2 is correction-data style; nothing else changes. Two repairs, both measured on the corpus before training: (1) v1 opened 12 of 31 viewer_reaction targets with an agreement clause and the adapter learned it as a formula, so v2 opens every one of them with a concrete fact instead and the agreement-opener count is now 0 of 31; (2) v1 satisfied the cue literal-emotion-keyword demand by naming the feeling, which taught the model to LABEL emotion, so v2 keeps the required keyword but attaches it to a body or action beat, raising slots with a physical beat from 8 of 32 to 25 of 32. next_hook targets are carried over unchanged because they were already varied and were not implicated. The keyword is retained deliberately rather than dropped: build_slotwise_cue demands it and slot_candidate_score ranks on it, so removing it would fight the selector and add a second variable. All 96 slots were re-verified through the runner own parse_slot_output with zero violations, zero honorifics and zero second-person address. Dataset v2 is 192 rows, 168 train / 12 dev / 12 test with the same turns 6 and 22 dev and 14 and 30 test holdout, 863,546 bytes, sha256 c81e4477f026beff3401fdc4c6bceb6aa0e36767c99b80dc426db15d513a3c1d. Training configuration is byte-identical to r2 — cuda-qlora, lora-r 16, lora-alpha 32, dropout 0.05, learning-rate 1e-4, max-steps 504, batch-size 1, gradient-accumulation 8, max-seq-len 1536, seed 42, same base weight pin 03755dea — into fresh adapter-r3, report and run-id. The dev curve is expected to behave as in r2 and the trainer will again publish the dev-selected state, so the effective dose stays about one epoch. PRE-REGISTERED EXPECTATION, recorded before the run so it cannot be revised afterwards: the alignment markers that r2 already closed should stay closed; manual items 3 and 4 should improve because both repairs target them directly; item 2 should NOT improve, because fabrication against the storyline and beat contradiction are comprehension failures that correction data of this kind cannot reach. If that is what the run shows, training has gone as far as this method goes and the next method is M8-9, the deterministic spine, which removes the comprehension requirement from the model rather than trying to teach it. Evaluation path is unchanged from r2 so the comparison stays single-variable: merge, package to Q4_K_M through the same pinned llama.cpp b10375 toolchain, create midm-airi-s3:eval-r3 from a Modelfile reproducing the operational template and parameters, and run the same fixed 32 turns. Operational tag untouched, adoption false, no commit and no push."
gpu_pc_stage3_eval_r2_receipt: "2026-09-01 19:07 KST: the post-training run completed, exit 0, 12.2 minutes, external root 20260901-vod-storyline-s3-r2, report sha256 3a51bbb77c48c995f762e617f04e8dccd668bd00f3eb35e368babbffeea3bbba. SINGLE-VARIABLE CONDITION IS VERIFIED, NOT ASSUMED: the evaluation tag midm-airi-s3:eval-r2 was created from a Modelfile reproducing ollama-proxy/Modelfile.midm-airi, and ollama create reported using existing layer for both the template blob sha256-8ebf7517 and the params blob sha256-4502a20b, so the only layer differing from the operational tag is the weights; the packaged Q4_K_M is 1,426,272,608 bytes against the operational 1,426,272,672. Knowledge stayed 153/296, digest pinned, cue set, storyline, sampling, validators and thresholds unchanged. AUTOMATIC FLOORS ALL PASS: 32/32 turns, 8/8 ordered scenes, signatures 0, empty/service errors 0, rewrites 32/32, repeated 0, maximum prior similarity 0.069, automatic source-experience 0, irrelevant knowledge 0, zero fallback drafts. THE ALIGNMENT AXIS MOVED HARD, and this is the first time in this project that a training round improved anything. Objective markers baseline to post over all 32 turns, my own regexes and therefore internally consistent but not comparable with the review PC estimators: question-mark turns 20 to 2, honorific turns 3 to 0, advice-register turns 8 to 0, second-person address 1 to 0, trailing ellipsis 2 to 0, demo-fragment leakage 4 hits in 3 turns to 0 in 0, third-person story subject 9 to 22 which is the intended direction. IT IS TRANSFER, NOT MEMORISATION, and this was pre-registered before the run by holding out turns 6, 22, 14 and 30 from training. The 4 held-out turns moved the same way as the 28 trained turns: question 4 to 0, honorific 1 to 0, advice 2 to 0, demo leakage 2 to 0, second person 1 to 0. Direct literal-reuse measurement against the 184 authored targets found EXACT 0 and NEAR 1 at ratio 0.81, so the adapter is not reciting the correction sentences. MANUAL 32-TURN 7-ITEM REVIEW STILL FAILS; consecutive passes remain 0/3. Items 6 and 7 now pass cleanly, item 1 mostly passes, item 5 nearly passes with two residuals the regex misses (T09 suggests the viewer take a rest, T15 directs the viewer), and items 2, 3 and 4 fail. What fails is not register, it is comprehension: (a) FABRICATION AGAINST THE STORYLINE at T07 which invents painkillers where the storyline has spicy food, T11 which renders the nodule warning as the throat being cut off, T14 which invents a doctor instruction about skipping candy with alcohol, T26 which says the person preferred being alone against a beat where fear drove the separation, T28 which attributes the isolation to the companions rather than the protagonist, and T30 and T32 which are simply incoherent; (b) BEAT CONTRADICTION and failure to reach the next hook at T12, T28 and T30; (c) IDEA-LEVEL REPETITION untouched, T05 says worried three times and T24 repeats its own opening clause, while the automatic repeat metric reports 0 and similarity 0.069, which is a third independent confirmation that the automatic detector cannot see idea repetition. TWO DEFECTS I INTRODUCED THROUGH DATA DESIGN, recorded as my own error rather than the model failing: my corrections overwhelmingly opened with an agreement clause, and the adapter learned that as a formula, so 그 말이 딱 맞아 and its variants now open many turns; and because the runner cue demands a literal emotion keyword, my targets named the emotion, so the model now LABELS emotion instead of enacting it, which is why the reference-only automatic emotion signal jumped 12/32 to 31/32 while manual item 3 got worse as craft. Any next data round must vary the opener and show emotion through action. VERDICT: training closed the alignment-shaped defects that four inference-time levers and full serving-layer control could not touch, and left the capability-shaped defects exactly where they were. That is the blocking point this goal asked me to identify. No adoption, adoption_authorized stays false, operational tag untouched, no commit and no push."
gpu_pc_stage3_r2_receipt: "2026-09-01 18:49 KST: r2 completed with exit 0 in 28.5 minutes, 504 microsteps and 63 optimizer updates over 3 epochs of the 168 training rows, peak CUDA memory 5,323,387,392 bytes on the 8 GiB card. THE DEV CURVE IS THE MAIN RESULT AND IT IS A CEILING SIGNAL, NOT A SUCCESS SIGNAL: training loss collapsed from a first-3 mean of 4.1244 to a last-3 mean of 0.5393, while held-out dev loss went 2.3157 at epoch 1, 2.6338 at epoch 2 and 3.0641 at epoch 3. Dev loss therefore MINIMISES AFTER ONE EPOCH and rises monotonically afterwards, which means 168 rows is small enough that the adapter starts memorising the corrected sentences rather than learning the register by epoch 2. The trainer selects on dev and restores the best state before publishing (restore_trainable_state on best_state at the end of the loop), so the published adapter is the EPOCH-1 state at dev 2.3157, not the overfit epoch-3 state; the effective dose is therefore one epoch, 21 optimizer updates, even though 63 were run. Recorded plainly because it bounds what this data can buy: more epochs on this corpus will not help, and any further gain has to come from more distinct corrected slots, not more passes. Artifacts: adapter-r2 with 28,114,944 trainable LoRA parameters and adapter_model.safetensors sha256 4f95d45854d02316c53d1cdb2a613b508ca1b1ce63fe3699025b11aeddf96629, dataset pin fc0ba951 and base weight pin 03755dea both re-verified by the trainer, training_authorization true, adoption_authorized false, t3 pending. MERGE RECEIPT: merge_airi_behavior_lora.py published merged-r2 with exit 0 in 1.6 minutes against the clean 9-file base copy (base artifact manifest 4ff13eb1, adapter artifact manifest 38a1b4bc under the merge own top-level rule, which is a different canonicalisation from the trainer own 2,199-byte artifact-manifest.json and had to be recomputed rather than copied). Merged model.safetensors is 4,611,084,960 bytes, sha256 ea70241b6fdb0a7982fec501b710edf542e009cc08e24e1268e493d489994733, with merge-evidence.json beside it. Packaging to Q4_K_M through the pinned llama.cpp b10375 converter and quantizer is running next. No adoption, no operational model change, no commit and no push."

gpu_pc_stage3_r1_underdose_receipt: "2026-09-01 18:20 KST: the first training run in this project completed with exit 0 in 4.9 minutes and is DISCARDED as under-dosed rather than evaluated, because reading the trainer loop after the fact showed I had mis-specified the dose. --max-steps counts MICROSTEPS, not optimizer steps: the loop increments step once per microbatch and only calls optimizer.step() when pending_microbatches reaches gradient-accumulation. With batch-size 1 and gradient-accumulation 8, my --max-steps 63 therefore bought 63 samples seen and 8 optimizer updates, which is 0.375 of one epoch over the 168 training rows, not the 3 epochs I intended and recorded in the intent. The run is real and its numbers are honest — train loss first-3 mean 4.1244 falling to last-3 mean 3.2781, dev loss 2.8169, 28,114,944 trainable LoRA parameters against 1,298,687,744 visible 4-bit parameters, peak CUDA memory 5,294,063,104 bytes, adapter artifact manifest sha256 005a04b51b9db6104338763a157cbe0133e9394a7c8c23a9bc23165c4f43a228 — but 8 optimizer updates on a 28 M-parameter adapter is not a dose that can be expected to move behaviour, so packaging and evaluating it would have burned about 25 minutes to measure noise. The artifact is kept at adapter-r1 as a dose-response data point and as evidence that the whole CUDA path works on this PC: model weight pin verified, dataset sha256 verified, dev and test holdouts accepted, VRAM sufficient at 7,970 of 8,192 MiB observed. What the run does establish, and this is worth recording: the trainer, the 4-bit path, the tokenizer and the dataset shape all work end to end on this GPU, and training authorization is true while adoption stays false with t3 pending."
gpu_pc_stage3_training_intent: "2026-09-01 18:05 KST: FIRST TRAINING RUN OF THIS PROJECT ON THIS PC (previous trainer count 0). Exact command shape: train_airi_behavior_lora.py --mode cuda-qlora with the dataset above pinned by its sha256, --model-dir the local Mi:dm HF snapshot (single model.safetensors, weight sha256 03755deae6cc183c9957bcf7b753924b0d1284e1b44aea95aaa822594765e70b, config.json present, no hub name and no network path), a fresh adapter output directory, a fresh report path, an immutable run-id and run-dir under the external Stage 3 root. Dose is deliberately small per the Small Model Learnability Gap prescription: lora-r 16, lora-alpha 32, lora-dropout 0.05, learning-rate 1e-4 (below the 2e-4 default), max-steps 63 which is about three epochs at batch-size 1 and gradient-accumulation 8, max-seq-len 1536 to cover the measured 1473-token maximum. Teacher is NOT enlarged: there is no distillation from a larger model, every target is a single short sentence in the register the runtime already serves, and 8 rows keep the base model own output so the adapter is anchored to its own distribution. Preconditions: the AIRI stack and Ollama are stopped first so the 8 GiB card is free, since the runtime model was holding 2,274,214,542 bytes of VRAM. Expected completion condition is a report with a falling loss and an adapter directory; the trainer refuses remote models, network paths, an existing output directory and unsafe CUDA conditions, and requires non-empty dev and test holdouts, which is why the split was changed from train/dev to train/dev/test before this run. NOTHING about the operational model changes: the midm-airi:2.0-mini tag and its pin are untouched, adoption stays false, operational flags stay off, and any evaluation of the trained artifact will go through the launcher supported evaluation model path under a separate tag. No commit and no push."
gpu_pc_stage3_dataset_receipt: "2026-09-01 18:00 KST: the Stage 3 correction dataset is built and machine-verified. It contains NO new synthetic scenario: every row is one slot of the fixed 32-turn storyline, and every target is either the baseline model output kept as-is or a single corrected sentence authored against the observed defect in that exact slot. Shape is the runtime shape, reconstructed offline without touching the proxy: message 1 is the immutable AIRI system prompt plus final contract, message 2 is the slot cue exactly as the proxy projects it through project_active_character_card (sanitized, prefixed Active Character Card), message 3 is the runner user content, message 4 is the target. A negative finding was measured on the way and is recorded rather than assumed: the slot cues are 448 to 779 characters against a 3900-character card budget, so the proxy does NOT truncate them and cue truncation is ruled out as a defect cause. Each slot contributes two rows because the runner uses build_slotwise_cue only on attempt 1 and build_slotwise_retry_cue on attempts 2 to 16, so the retry cue is 15 of 16 of the real call distribution. EVERY authored target was verified through the runner own parse_slot_output against its own beat, which caught and forced the repair of five of my own drafts (staging-meta 방송 중 and 장면에서, a missing literal emotion term, a 14-character beat recitation, and a SOURCE_EXPERIENCE hit on 나와); the final corpus is 96/96 slots accepted with zero violations, zero honorifics and zero second-person address. Totals: 192 rows, 168 train / 12 dev / 12 test, 8 kept model outputs and 184 authored corrections, 864,632 bytes, sha256 fc0ba951f6702202dc762775a2c3cfd5818e436985c2abb58a6c7924fd92ad10, stored outside the repository. Turns 6 and 22 are the dev holdout and turns 14 and 30 the test holdout, chosen one per act pair and excluded from training so the post-training run measures generalisation to beats the adapter never saw rather than memorisation. Token lengths under the model tokenizer are min 1137, median 1275, max 1473. The run-89 and run-92 receipts corroborate the defect categories but contribute no rows, because their transcripts are on the review PC and their beats are the same fixed storyline already covered here."

claude_pc_run92_receipt: "2026-09-01 15:3x KST: run-92 (Stage 2, serving-layer token suppression; external root C:\\AIRI-Models\\airi-human-eval\\20260901-vod-storyline-run-92, report SHA256 8dd93c3aace81469720d56eb72c2076a4196f0d707e8b11e33107bbf7eeecd54) completed 32/32 exit 0, floors clean, rewrites 32/32, automatic gate still false (new_event false, emotion false). The pre-registered guarantee HELD exactly: '?' fell from 11 turns in run-89 to 0 across all 32 turns — a structural, not statistical, elimination. Everything else the pre-registration predicted also held, and two findings go against the lever. (1) Honorifics were NOT zeroed: 1 turn in run-89 and still 1 in run-92, because T01 says '꿈을 꾸셨구나' and the 534-id set covered 습니다/입니다/합니다/세요/으신/으시는 but not the 시/셨 stem contraction — a completeness gap in my enumeration, recorded rather than claimed as success. (2) Suppressing the character did not suppress the speech act: manual reading found interrogatives surviving as unterminated run-ons (T02 '말해줄 수 있어, 오늘은 무슨 꿈이야', T13 '그 캔디는 어떤 맛이야', T21, T24), and trailing ellipsis endings appeared where none existed before (0 turns in run-89 -> 3 in run-92 at T22/T28/T30). A narrow interrogative regex scored 0 on both runs and is reported as inadequate rather than as evidence. Advice register measured 2 -> 4 turns (T07 '병원 바로 가는 게 제일 안전하니까', T14, T17, T27), and T07 additionally invented '의사가 매운 음식 때문에 위장이 많이 상했다고', contradicting the throat storyline. Role inversion persisted or worsened: T01 asks the viewer about the viewer's own dream, T27 instructs the viewer, T30 says '우리도 안심이'. Manual 32-turn review FAILS; consecutive quality passes remain 0/3."
claude_pc_stage2_conclusion: "2026-09-01 15:40 KST: STAGE 2 IS EXHAUSTED and, with Stage 1, closes every inference-time and serving-time lever available on this machine. The combined result is the cleanest statement of the ceiling so far: when the serving layer is fully controlled, form obeys and meaning does not. Token suppression can make a surface character impossible ('?' 11 -> 0) but cannot remove the intent behind it — the model re-expressed the same interrogative act as malformed run-ons and ellipses — and it cannot touch implicit self-experience, advice framing, role inversion, beat contradiction or same-idea repetition, which together remain the dominant residual across run-89 and run-92 alike. Remaining M8 stages are Stage 3 (corrective fine-tuning of Mi:dm per the Small Model Learnability Gap prescription) and Stage 4 (model-axis diagnosis, deprioritized by the user to protect Korean ability). Stage 3 CANNOT run on this PC: it has no CUDA device, so a GPU PC is now the gating resource — this is the explicit GPU notification the user asked for. Infrastructure left in place and reusable: llama_server_shim.py plus its 18 offline tests, the measured 534-id suppression set, the GGUF vocabulary dump procedure, and the finding that Ollama's bundled llama-server already provides logit_bias/grammar without any new install. Nothing operational changed, adoption stays false, and no commit or push has been made."
claude_pc_stage2_parity_defect_and_fix: "2026-09-01 12:3x-13:0x KST: the delegated worker's completion report flagged, and read-only inspection of ollama_proxy.py CONFIRMED, that the proxy stamps OLLAMA_SAMPLING_DEFAULTS (temperature 0.45, top_p 0.9, repeat_penalty 1.05) onto every request while the shim's option mapping only relayed temperature, seed, stop and num_predict. Dropping top_p and repeat_penalty would have let llama-server apply its own defaults (0.95 / 1.0), so the first Stage 2 attempt was NOT a single-variable experiment. It was stopped at 0.7 minutes with no report and carries zero evidentiary value; the run root was abandoned. Fix: a red/green test asserting all three sampling values reach the OpenAI body (and that absent values stay absent rather than being invented) now guards the mapping, the shim relays top_p and repeat_penalty, and 18 shim tests pass with py_compile and diff-check clean. Second defect found by live probe: the running llama-server still carried only the first 14 --logit-bias entries, not the 534 enumerated later, which is why '안녕하세요' (id 7602) and a merged '?!' token still appeared — the flags were working, the set was simply stale. llama-server was restarted with all 534 ids (1,079 argv entries) against the same pinned weights blob. Post-fix live verification through proxy -> shim -> llama-server: an explicit honorific request returned no honorific and an explicit question request returned no '?', while an advice request still produced advice in 반말, exactly matching the pre-registered expectation that suppression closes form and not meaning."

claude_pc_stage2_bias_measurement: "2026-09-01 11:4x KST: the Stage 2 suppression set is now measured rather than guessed, and the measurement changed the design twice. First attempt blocked 14 standalone token ids (조심 30695, 안전 2515, 습니다 588, 세요 1805 …) and FAILED live: the model still produced '좋습니다', '없습니다' and '안녕하세요'. Cause measured: this is byte-level BPE and a leading space produces a different merge, so ' 좋습니다' is a single id 6623, ' 없습니다' is 4020 and '안녕하세요' is 7602 — none of which the standalone ids cover. Enumerating surface variants by hand is unbounded, so the vocabulary was dumped directly from the pinned GGUF's tokenizer.ggml.tokens metadata (131,392 entries) with a purpose-written reader, and the pieces were decoded through the GPT-2 byte-to-unicode inverse (verified: id 588='습니다', 6623=' 좋습니다', 7602='안녕하세요'). Final set = 534 ids: 234 carrying '?' or '？', and 300 carrying 습니다/입니다/합니다/세요/십시오/으신/으시는/십니다/시죠/시네요. Bare '니다' was deliberately excluded because it also matches plain 반말 '아니다'. Collateral check against required narration vocabulary (무서웠어, 쫓아왔어, 거울, 줄무늬, 도망쳤어, 그 사람, 주인공, 아니다, 한다 …) reports zero conflicts. llama-server accepts logit_bias both per request and as --logit-bias startup flags, and is confirmed running on 11500 against the same pinned weights blob. SCOPE HONESTY, recorded before the run so it cannot be claimed afterwards: token suppression can guarantee zero only for question endings and honorifics — about 4-5 of run-89's 32 turns. The dominant residuals, implicit self-experience (~8 turns) and advice register (~4 turns), are semantic and were measured to route around token blocks by choosing other wording, so Stage 2 alone is not expected to yield a passing transcript; its value is a hard guarantee on two of the seven manual criteria plus decisive evidence for the ceiling conclusion. Shim implementation is in progress in a delegated worker and will be re-verified here before any run."
claude_pc_stage2_design_intent: "2026-09-01 10:20 KST: user goal 'STAGE2 진행'. Design settled and it requires ZERO production-proxy changes, which removes the risk flagged in the earlier recon. Key facts measured today: llama-server's --logit-bias and --grammar are STARTUP flags, so no per-request plumbing is needed; Ollama's /api/chat stream is application/x-ndjson whose lines are {model, created_at, message:{role,content}, done:false} with a terminal line adding done_reason, total_duration, load_duration, prompt_eval_count, prompt_eval_duration, eval_count, eval_duration — captured live from the pinned model. Therefore Stage 2 is implemented as a NEW evaluation-only shim under ollama-proxy/eval/ that listens on 11434 (after Ollama is stopped), speaks Ollama-native /api/chat and /api/tags to the unmodified proxy, and forwards to llama-server's OpenAI-shaped /v1/chat/completions on 11500 where the logit_bias is already applied at startup. The AIRI proxy, its immutable prompt, the broadcast contract, knowledge 144/287 and every runtime flag stay byte-identical, so the only changed variable is the generation constraint at the serving layer. Model weights are the same pinned GGUF blob sha256:31beb9a880b2…, so the artifact is unchanged too. Implementation is delegated to a worker with red/green discipline and will be re-verified here by test run and diff review before any storyline run. Target defect: the residual surface register — advice phrasing, question endings, honorifics — which Stage 1 could reduce but never zero. No adoption, no operational change, no commit or push."
claude_pc_run90_receipt_and_m8_4_discard: "2026-08-29 03:1x KST: run-90 (M8-4 depth injection plus anti-impersonation; external root ...run-90, report SHA256 9088f7639ba2962aa0d0dbf92ad2fa548e4e3bcba57f82761f4300be7f2c9cb2) completed 32/32 exit 0 with clean floors and 32/32 rewrites, but is NET NEGATIVE against run-89 on every axis that moved: demo-fragment leakage 2 hits in 1 turn -> 7 hits in 3 turns (T01, T02, T26), 그사람-or-주인공 subject 10 -> 8, question endings 4 -> 7, and the automatic all-turns new-event signal true -> false; advice register and honorific leakage held at 2 and 0. Reading: appending more trailing instruction dilutes a 2.3B model's attention and pushes it toward copying the nearest surface pattern, which is the demo — consistent with the ICL literature that more instruction/demo material can hurt small models. Per the pre-registered lever rule M8-4 is DISCARDED and SLOT_DEPTH_REMINDER is set to an empty string with the contract kept explicit in tests rather than silently deleted. The revert itself introduced and then caught a real bug: build_slotwise_retry_cue stripped the reminder with base[:-len(reminder)], and with an empty reminder endswith('') is always true so the slice wiped the entire cue; the existing M8-2 framing test failed on it, the guard is now non-empty-only, and 759 tests pass with 1 skipped, py_compile and diff-check clean. STAGE 1 IS NOW EXHAUSTED: 1a CRANE isolation discarded, 1b story-character framing kept, 1c per-slot demos kept in corrected form, 1d depth injection discarded. Best configuration is the run-89 form, which the runner now reproduces byte-for-byte. Manual quality passes remain 0/3 — no Stage 1 lever produced a passing transcript, and residual defects are concentrated in surface register (advice, question endings), implicit self-experience, demo leakage and occasional malformed output. This satisfies the M8-6 resume condition, so Stage 2 (llama-server logit_bias plus minimal end-of-sentence GBNF) is the next stage; per the Stage 2 recon it requires a default-off evaluation-seam change routing local chat to the OpenAI-shaped upstream, since the proxy currently drives Ollama-native /api/chat which llama-server does not implement."

## 4. 다음 허용 행동

1. M8 Stage 순서대로 실행한다: M8-1(CRANE식 story 모드) 구현·red/green → Mi:dm 32턴
   fresh run → 자동 바닥 지표와 32턴 수동 7항목 검토 분리 보고. 레버 판정 규칙(§2)에 따라
   조합 누적 또는 폐기를 결정하고 각 회차 전후 intent/receipt를 남긴다.
2. Stage 순서를 위반하지 않는다 — 모델 축(M8-8)은 Stage 1~3 소진 전 시작 금지(사용자
   우선순위: Mi:dm 한국어 능력 유지). 운영 채택·live capability는 3연속 통과 후에도 별도
   사용자 결정이다. 실제 채팅 원문·식별 정보·응답 report는 저장소·문서에 넣지 않는다.
3. commit/push는 별도 사용자 승인 뒤에만 수행한다. 검증기 적층은 재개하지 않는다.

## 5. 갱신 트리거

active goal에서는 다음 중 하나라도 발생하면 이 파일을 먼저 갱신한다.

- 마지막 기록 후 최대 120분 경과(heartbeat 상한)
- 체크리스트 항목 또는 테스트 묶음 완료·실패
- 10분 이상 걸릴 수 있는 명령 실행 직전과 종료 직후
- 장기 프로세스 시작·PID 변경·중단·checkpoint 생성
- 새 산출물·해시·커밋·push·권한·blocker 발생
- 사용자의 pause/stop/resume, 세션 종료, 예상 가능한 재부팅
- compact가 예상되거나, 요약된 컨텍스트를 받았다고 판단한 직후

자동 compact 직전 알림은 보장되지 않는다. 따라서 “compact 직전 기록”에만
의존하지 않고 위 이벤트 기록과 60분 heartbeat를 함께 사용한다. heartbeat는
이 문서만 짧게 덮어쓰며, 로드맵 로그는 milestone에서만 갱신한다.
GPU 학습·merge/package·T3·장기 campaign 중 heartbeat 상한은 30분이다.

## 6. 시작·resume·compact 후 복구 절차

1. **행동 전에 이 파일 전체를 읽는다.**
2. 실제 goal status와 최신 사용자 명령을 확인해 권한 경계를 복원한다.
3. `git status --short --branch`, HEAD, 관련 PID·command line, 산출물·로그 크기와
   SHA를 read-only로 확인한다.
4. 현행 GPU 인계서와 로드맵 체크리스트의 해당 단계만 다시 읽는다.
5. 관측 상태와 이 문서가 다르면 실행하지 말고 이 문서를 실제 상태로 정정하며
   차이를 로드맵 로그에 남긴다.
6. 이미 실행 중인 PID가 있으면 exact command/output/log를 확인하기 전에는
   중복 프로세스를 시작하지 않는다.
7. 복구 시각·관측 근거·다음 한 동작을 새 checkpoint ID로 기록한 뒤 작업한다.

충돌 시 우선순위는 `최신 사용자 명령·goal status → 실제 프로세스/파일/SHA →
이 live state → 현행 handoff → roadmap status/log → compact된 채팅 요약`이다.

## 7. 단계 전후 기록 형식

장기·상태 변경 작업은 한 번의 서술로 끝내지 않고 두 단계로 기록한다.

- **intent checkpoint:** 실행 전 권한, exact command, 입력 SHA, 출력 경로,
  기대 완료 조건, 중단/복구 방식을 기록한다.
- **receipt checkpoint:** 종료 후 exit code, PID 종료 여부, 산출물 크기/SHA,
  테스트 결과, 실패·부분 완료 여부와 다음 한 동작을 기록한다.

의도만 있고 receipt가 없으면 완료가 아니다. 계산 시간이 있었더라도 checkpoint나
검증 산출물이 없으면 진척으로 승격하지 않는다.

## 8. 장기 문서로 승격하는 시점

- checklist 항목 완료·실패, 권한 변경, 모델/데이터/산출물 SHA 변경:
  현행 handoff와 roadmap status를 갱신한다.
- 매 작업 배치·commit: `AIRI-ROADMAP-LOG.md`에 기록한다.
- pause·handoff·PC 전환: 이 파일을 최종 receipt 상태로 갱신하고, 허가된 경우에만
  commit/push한다. push하지 못했으면 로컬 전용 상태임을 사용자에게 명시한다.
- heartbeat만 발생: 이 파일만 갱신하며 장기 문서를 불필요하게 다시 쓰지 않는다.

비밀, credential/token, `.env` 값, 원문 방송 데이터, 개인정보·개인 경로, 개인
오디오, 모델 weight, 런타임 DB와 로그 본문은 이 문서에 기록하지 않는다. exact
command는 민감한 인자를 redaction하고 경로·크기·비민감 SHA와 판정만 기록한다.
입력 clean preflight에서는 heartbeat로 생긴 이 파일 단독 diff만 명시적으로 제외해
별도 검토할 수 있다. 다른 tracked/untracked 변경은 허용하지 않는다.
